It's been pretty steady, actually. Just... that same heavy feeling every day. I haven't really noticed any specific days where it suddenly got a lot worse than usual. It's mostly just been a constant thing for the whole five months.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not really. Um, I actually stopped going to my salsa class a while ago. And I used to paint, but... well, my paints are still sitting out on the table at home. I just look at them and feel nothing. It's almost like they belong to somebody else now. So, no, I haven't really been enjoying anything lately.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in activities that you used to enjoy, or is it more that you're just not feeling any pleasure when you do them?\n\nPatient: I guess, like I was just saying, it's mostly that I've completely lost the interest to even try. I don't go to the salsa class at all anymore, so I'm not even doing it to see if I'd enjoy it. And with the painting... I don't even pick up the brushes. They just sit there on the table. I look at them and just feel completely disconnected, like it's someone else's stuff. I just don't have the drive to even start doing them in the first place.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant changes in your appetite or weight since you started feeling this heavy, empty feeling?\n\nPatient: Yeah, my appetite has definitely been off lately. I keep finding myself just skipping dinner entirely, or... maybe I'll just eat a few crackers while I'm looking over my budget notebook. I haven't actually weighed myself, but I know I've lost something because my work pants feel a lot looser than they did a few weeks ago.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you're moving more slowly than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. It just takes me longer to get going. But then, other times, it's almost the opposite. I'll find myself fidgeting with a stack of receipts and I just can't sit still at my desk. So it's kind of a mix of both, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt this heavy, empty feeling before the past five months?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, not like this. This is the first time I've felt this down and just... empty for this long.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you used to enjoy, but now find pleasure in only some of the time?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. I just don't have any drive to do those things anymore.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in, even if it's just a little bit?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I've thought about it, but honestly... nothing sounds good right now. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of death or suicide since you started feeling this heavy, empty feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I feel really awful and empty, but... I haven't thought about dying or anything like that. I definitely don't want to hurt myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt even worse than you do now, but then improved somewhat before feeling badly again?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's just been a steady kind of awful since it started. I haven't really noticed it getting better and then worse again, just... consistently bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any difficulty concentrating on things like reading or watching TV?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. It's actually been really noticeable at work. I do bookkeeping, and the spreadsheets I used to handle easily just kind of blur together now. It's just really hard to focus on anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt slightly better, even if just for a day or two, since this heavy, empty feeling started?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Like I was saying a minute ago, it hasn't really let up at all. It just takes me longer to get going. But then, other times, it's almost the opposite. I'll find myself fidgeting with a stack of receipts and I just can't sit still at my desk. So it's kind of a mix of both, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt this heavy, empty feeling before the past five months?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, not like this. This is the first time I've felt this down and just... empty for this long.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you used to enjoy, but now find pleasure in only some of the time?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. I used to really enjoy a few things that I just don't care about right now. Like, I completely stopped going to my salsa class. And my paints are actually still sitting out on the table at home, but... I don't know, I look at them and I just feel nothing. It's weird, it's almost like they belong to somebody else. I just don't have any drive to do those things anymore.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in, even if it's just a little bit?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I've thought about it, but honestly... nothing sounds good right now. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of death or suicide since you started feeling this heavy, empty feeling?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I feel really awful and empty, but... I haven't thought about dying or anything like that. I definitely don't want to hurt myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt even worse than you do now, but then improved somewhat before feeling badly again?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's just been a steady kind of awful since it started. I haven't really noticed it getting better and then worse again, just... consistently bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any difficulty concentrating on things like reading or watching TV?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. It's actually been really noticeable at work. I do bookkeeping, and the spreadsheets I used to handle easily just kind of blur together now. I'll find myself rereading the same row of numbers over and over... and even then, I still don't trust what I actually entered. It's just really hard to focus on anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt slightly better, even if just for a day or two, since this heavy, empty feeling started?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Like I was saying a minute ago, it hasn't really let up at all. I haven't had any good days or even a day where it felt a little lighter... it's just been that same heavy feeling the whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of at least two weeks where you felt even slightly better since this heavy, empty feeling started?\n\nPatient: No, like I mentioned, I really haven't.
